===== [p336-336] b. Function for Correcting Short-term Fluctuation in Focus (2块) =====
--[p336]-- In case of focus calibration performed by the EXECUTE, CONTINUE, or READY command, even if executing the above command, exposure starts without performing focus calibration. At that time, if the focus calibration result obtained by the CALIBRATE command is saved in the system, the result is used for focus calibration (NOTE 1).
--[p336]-- Therefore, under the imaging system condition of the small σ illumination, performing focus calibration using the EXECUTE, CONTINUE, or READY command is not generally recommended (NOTE 2) .

===== [p20-20] 2.2 PROJECTION SYSTEM (3块) =====
--[p20]-- The lens controller controls the reduction projection lens to provide the optimum magnification and focus. The major causes of fluctuations in magnification and focus in the reduction projection lens are changes in atmospheric pressure and in the temperature of the projection lens.
The reduction projection lens can be divided into two main chambers: the so-called room A and room B.
--[p20]-- These fluctuations are compensated by controlling the pressure of chamber room B (refractive index of air). The fluctuations on focus are compensated by the specification in the auto focus unit.
The operator can verify the atmospheric pressure and the pressure in chamber room B at the operation panel.
--[p20]-- Room A is the part of the lens that is left open to make its pressure the same as the one in the chamber (atmospheric pressure). Room B is the space between two lenses that is connected with the pressure control unit.
The lens controller continuously calculates the fluctuations in magnification and focus from the following data: atmospheric pressure, temperature in the chambers, exposure energy, and shutter opening and closing times.

===== [p264-264] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 7.5 SHOT MAGNIFICATION CORRECTION (3块) =====
--[p264]-- Variations in the environment such as temperature and atmospheric pressure can change the magnification of the reduction projection lens so that in the NSR, by estimating the variations, the exposure magnification can be constantly maintained at a fixed level.
Actual wafers and reticles, however, suffer from variations in expansion and contraction.
--[p264]-- To achieve this, the NSR implements shot magnification correction.
In the shot magnification correction, two items concerning the shot size are taken into account, and in exposure, the amount of lens magnification correction is sent to the lens controller where the magnification of the projection lens is intentionally controlled in the following manner:
--[p264]-- Actual wafers and reticles, however, suffer from variations in expansion and contraction.
This requires not only keeping the projected image the same size as the design specification, but in some cases adjusting the projection magnification of the reduction projection lens so that the projected image becomes the proper size. To achieve this, the NSR implements shot magnification correction.

===== [p26-26] 3.4 WAFER STAGE INTERFEROMETERS (2块) =====
--[p26]-- The wafer stage interferometers are distance meters using a laser to control the coordinates of the wafer stage with high accuracy.
The X and Y coordinates and the rotation of the wafer stage are precisely measured by using three interferometers, which are two on the X axis and one on the Y axis.
Wafer stage interferometers WX1, WX2, and WY are positioned as shown in the following figure.
--[p26]-- Wafer stage interferometers WX1, WX2, and WY are positioned as shown in the following figure.
The array of shots exposed on the wafer is affected by the orthogonality and flatness of the X and Y movable mirrors. That’s why the NSR incorporates functions for correcting these components. The wafer stage interferometers employ one interferometer laser for its exclusive use.

===== [p211-211] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 3. Calculate the mark center from the peak waveform. (1块) =====
--[p211]-- The final slice level is computed from the signal waveform for each peak (peak waveform) derived in step 2 according to the signal processing algorithm and slice level that have been specified. The peak waveform is sliced at the final slice level to determine the mark center.
The signal processing algorithm and the slice level are specified using LSA Signal Processing Algorithm (p) and LSA Algorithm Slice Level (p).

===== [p315-315] • Best level when leveling is not performed (1块) =====
--[p315]-- In this case, the best level is the plane adjusted so that the image plane becomes parallel to the wafer holder.
This best level is calculated by the following formula based on the leveling reset plane adjusted so that the wafer stage travel plane is parallel to the wafer holder.
Best level = (Leveling reset plane) + (Leveling Offset (Off) (c)/(p)) + (Leveling Offset Adjustment (Off)(m))

===== [p226-226] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Leveling in EGA Measurement (1块) =====
--[p226]-- The shot face gradient in EGA measurement is fixed to the level (leveling reset plane) where the wafer holder is parallel to the travel plane of the wafer stage.

===== [p238-238] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > EGA Random Factor Tolerance (s) (2块) =====
--[p238]-- When an EGA limit error occurs, depending on the situation, the following functions start to prevent the error from interrupting processing:
• Automatic OF retry
• Automatic reject
For additional information on them, see the next section.
--[p238]-- Standard setting: “0.0” [µm]
An EGA limit error occurs if the width of the variation (3σ: three times the standard deviation) in the values derived by removing linear factors from measurement result of each EGA mark fails to satisfy the following formula:
3σ ≤ (EGA Random Factor Tolerance (s))
Specify this parameter separately for g-EGA and t-EGA. The width of the variation cannot be checked with this parameter, however, if “0.0” [µm] is specified.

===== [p48-48] ◆ 1 to 26, T1 to T9 (1块) =====
--[p48]-- Valid numbers vary depending on the configuration of the reticle library. T1 through T9 are slots for test reticles. They can be specified when the test slots are defined in the reticle library.
There are two ways of using the reticle library.

===== [p109-109] (4) LSA Sensor (Laser Step Alignment Sensor) (2块) =====
--[p109]-- By this operation, a laser spot passes across grid-type marks, thereby causing diffracted light.
Among the diffracted light, only the ±1st-order diffracted light is led to the signal processor.
The following graph indicates the relationship between the wafer stage position and the signal strength at the measurement.
In the wafer alignment mark measurement using LSA sensors, the peak of the signal in this graph is regarded as the time when the wafer alignment mark passed across the laser spot.
Then, with the wafer stage position, the wafer alignment mark position on the wafer stage coordinate system can be calculated.
--[p109]-- The LSA sensors are used to measure the position of the wafer alignment mark. These sensors are used in the wafer search alignment and EGA.
In the measurement, the wafer stage is moved to scan LSA marks on the condition that a slit-shape laser spot (He-Ne laser beam) reduced through the projection lens is focused onto the wafer surface.
By this operation, a laser spot passes across grid-type marks, thereby causing diffracted light.

===== [p215-215] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ◆ Excluded (1块) =====
--[p215]-- The multimark-end signals are not used. After all of the peaks of an N-line multimark are detected, the peak waveforms of both ends are excluded, and the corresponding to the remainder of the lines (N-2) are used to calculate the mark position.

===== [p315-315] [1] Leveling Offset Adjustment (Off) (m) (1块) =====
--[p315]-- Performs an evaluation exposure to derive the amount of displacement in the inclination of the image plane, an amount that is unique to the NSR, when the leveling reset plane is the reference.

===== [p188-188] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Indirect shot factor application model (2块) =====
--[p188]-- Because the indirect shot factor application model uses the 10-parameter model to calculate the shot factors, conditions necessary for applying this model are the same as those for the 10-parameter model.
--[p188]-- This model uses the 6-parameter model to compute the wafer factors and the 10- parameter model to compute the shot factors. The wafer factors are calculated solely from the measurement results of the first measurement point in each EGA shot.
The shot factors are derived from all the measurement results at multiple measurement points in each shot (NOTE 3) . The wafer offset factor is corrected on the basis of the offset factor computed from the shot factors.

===== [p50-51] 3.1 PURPOSE OF RETICLE CHECK (4块) =====
--[p50]-- This function uses a barcode reader to scan the barcode written on the reticle and then judges from the information on the barcode whether the correct reticle is being loaded.
NOTE
1. The reticle particle check requires an optional particle check unit.
2. The reticle ID check requires an optional barcode reader.
The reticle check carried out during reticle loading determines whether a reticle can be used in exposure.
--[p51 [表]]-- [1] | Reticle Loading Check Switch (c)/(s) | Sequence Switch
Specifies whether to perform reticle particle check during reticle loading.
◆ No Loading Check Does not perform reticle particle check during reticle loading.
◆ Loading Check Performs reticle particle check during reticle loading.
--[p50]-- The one performed during reticle unloading verifies the reliability of the exposure results using that reticle.
To execute reticle particle check during reticle loading, set Reticle Loading Check Switch (c)/(s) to “Loading Check”. To perform ID check, set Reticle Identification Check Switch (c)/(s) to “Identify”.
To execute reticle particle check during reticle unloading, set Reticle Unloading Check Switch (c)/(s) to “Unloading Check”.
--[p50]-- There are two types of reticle check: reticle ID check and reticle particle check.
The reticle particle check (NOTE 1) examines the surfaces of the reticle and pellicle for particles, and determines whether it can be used in exposure. This ensures that particles on the reticle will not be transferred to the wafer. If the reticle fails in the check, it must be cleaned or replaced.
The reticle ID check (NOTE 2) is performed to prevent an incorrect reticle from being used in exposure.

===== [p111-112] (6) LIA Sensor (Laser Interferometer Alignment Sensor) (4块) =====
--[p112]-- In case of using LIA sensors, the wafer alignment mark position shift should be entered within ±P/4 of design position of the mark beforehand. When performing EGA by LIA in the process where search alignment accuracy is unstable, perform t-EGA by LIA after performing g-EGA by LSA or FIA.
--[p111]-- In this case, two-beam laser means the beam which has two kinds of frequencies, f1 and f2 (f1- f2 = 25 kHz), changed by the frequency shifter. Two-beam laser is radiated with the incident angle so that +1st-order diffracted beam of f1 and -1st-order diffracted beam of f2 generated on each mark may overlap each other in the vertical direction.
The overlapped diffracted light generates a beat signal with frequency, f1- f2 (25 kHz), which has the position information on each grid mark.
--[p111]-- The beat signal generated at the wafer alignment mark is called “wafer signal” and the one generated at the reference mark is called “reference signal.”
The alignment mark shift is shown as phase contrast of the wafer signal from the reference signal.
With this shift and the wafer stage position at the measurement, the wafer alignment mark position on the wafer stage coordinate system can be calculated.
--[p111]-- The LIA sensors are the ones using two-beam heterodyne technology for the measurement of the wafer alignment mark position. These sensors are used in EGA.
In the measurement, grid-type wafer alignment marks are placed under LIA sensors. With the wafer stage stopped, non-photosensitive two-beam laser (He-Ne laser) illuminates marks on the wafer and the same grid-type reference mark fixed to NSR.

• Exchanging reticles for exposure (loading and unloading)

There are two types of reticle exchange:
Change & Alignment The reticle on the stage is replaced by the specified reticle, and alignment is performed.

===== [p218-219] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 4. Calculate the mark center from the edges of the signal detected in step 3. (2块) =====
--[p218]-- There are two types of signals (signal profiles) captured by the FIA sensor: a single type (light-dark-light) and a double type (dark-light-dark). The type that is actually captured depends on the type and conditions of the process.
The edges used to compute the mark center varies with the signal profile. When the signal profile is a double type, it is necessary to specify which edges will be used: the two inner edges or the two outer edges.
--[p219]-- Specify the signal profile and the edges that will be used to compute the mark center using FIA Signal Profile (p) and Edge Selection Mode (p).
Use the midpoint detection method as an algorithm to calculate the center of the mark from the edge. To do this, specify “41” in FIA Signal Processing Algorithm (p). For multimarks, all the results derived at the peak corresponding to each mark line are averaged to produce the measurement result.

===== [p276-276] • Lens magnification control (1块) =====
--[p276]-- The lens magnification is adjusted according to the following values: the scaling factors derived in wafer alignment and the reticle elasticity amount due to irradiation by exposure light. ===== [p212-212] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ● Midpoint difference method (algorithm 3 or 5) (2块) =====
--[p212]-- As shown in the examples given in the above figure, C is set as the final slice level within levels A, B, and C, where the left and right slopes are similar and stable. C indicates the point at which the center coordinate of the waveform most closely approaches the center coordinate of the specified slice level.
--[p212]-- This method sets the level that satisfies the following requirements as the final slice level. The center coordinate of the peak waveform at this level must be near the center coordinate of the specified slice level. And besides, the left and right gradients of the peak waveform must be equal stably at this slice level. The peak waveform is sliced at the final slice level and the mark center is derived.
Center Coordinate of Specified Slice Level

===== [p177-177] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > 5.7.1 Automatic Recovery Processing for an Error in Wafer Search Alignment (3块) =====
--[p177]-- ExecutionOrder | Recovery Technique | Function
6 | Automatic reject | The wafer in which the error occurred is rejected, and processinggoes on to the next processing.
The type of process and sequence of execution, however, vary depending on the kind of error that occurs, as follows:
• Alignment mark detection error (measurement error) 1→2→3→4→5→6
• Wafer rotation tolerance over
• Focusing error
--[p177 [表]]-- When an error occurs in wafer search alignment, the NSR executes automatic recovery operations such as re-scanning the alignment mark in order to avoid an interruption in the processing due to an user assist. When an error is generated, automatic recovery processes are executed in the following sequence:
ExecutionOrder | Recovery Technique | Function
1 | Automaticremeasurement | When a measurement error occurs, measurement is re-executed.
--[p177]-- ExecutionOrder | Recovery Technique | Function
2 | Automatic extension ofthe search range | If automatic remeasurement fails, the search range is extended andremeasurement is performed.
3 | Around search (wafersearch alignment) | If measurement fails with "automatic extension of the search range,"shots located adjacent to the target shots are regarded as s-shotsand measured instead.
4 | Automatic OF retry | Wafer alignment is retried from pre-alignment_1.
5 | Assist | Recovery processing is carried out by the user.

===== [p194-194] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > If g-EGA and t-EGA are executed for the purpose of combining correction factors from two types of sensors to get high accuracy (1块) =====

These selected factors become the final correction factors. For convenience, the correction function (composed of these final correction factors) is expressed as Fdesign→EGA. ===== [p298-298] Chapter 6 FOCUSING AND LEVELING (2块) =====
--[p298]-- This ensures that the image of the reticle pattern is correctly joined in each shot.
The process of adjusting the height of the shot surface to the focal plane of the reduction projection lens is referred to as focusing, and the process of adjusting the inclination of the shot surface to the image plane of the lens is called leveling.
This chapter describes focusing. For information on focusing that takes place in wafer alignment, see Part IV, “Alignment” of this manual.
--[p298]-- In exposure, the height of the shot surface must be adjusted to the focal plane of the reduction projection lens. Then, the inclination of the shot surface must be adjusted to the image plane of the projection lens. This ensures that the image of the reticle pattern is correctly joined in each shot.

===== [p318-319] \~ Shot Criteria for Normal Leveling \~ (3块) =====
--[p318]-- Measured signals obtained from such points can hardly be expected to yield adequate leveling.
The area on the wafer in which leveling is impossible is normally 9.5 mm (standard setting (NOTE) ) from the edge of the wafer. Depending on the state of the wafers for each process, leveling feasible ranges may be changed by Leveling Disable Range (p).
NOTE
The standard setting is determined for each machine.
Standard setting: “0”
This parameter specifies the width of the focusing unfeasible range (measured from the wafer edge).
Specifying “0” will enable the machine’s default setting.
--[p318]-- In order to correctly perform leveling based on the measurement by the leveling sensor, the shot must be located in the leveling feasible area where is inside the wafer edge. If the shot is on the edge of the wafer, the points measured by the leveling sensor will lie on a non-uniform wafer surface or even off the wafer. Measured signals obtained from such points can hardly be expected to yield adequate leveling.
--[p319]-- Specifying “0” will enable the machine’s default setting.
The possibility of leveling is judged for each shot. The outcome depends on whether the leveling sensor lies in the leveling feasible area (whether it lies inside the area extending from the edge of the wafer by a width specified by Leveling Disable Range (p)).
In order to perform leveling, focusing (or shift focusing) must be performed at the shot in advance. Leveling, which includes shift leveling, cannot be performed for the shot on which focusing has not been performed.

===== [p288-288] • Stepping group (1块) =====
--[p288]-- This refers to a group of shot elements that determines the stepping sequence. Shot elements of each shot defined on the wafer shot map can be grouped together to improve the efficiency of exposure processing. The sequence of exposure within a stepping group depends on the stepping route.

===== [p264-264] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Reticle elasticity control (1块) =====
--[p264]-- Correction for eliminating expansion of the reticle
These adjustments ensure that shots are always maintained at the proper size in the first exposure. In the second exposure, the size of shots to be exposed is correctly overlaid on that of the shots on the previous layer.

===== [p252-252] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Shot Scaling Correction (p) (1块) =====
--[p252]-- Specifies a correction value for the shot scaling factor. The direction of expansion is a positive value.

===== [p176-176] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > (2) Leveling in Search Alignment (1块) =====
--[p176]-- The level in search alignment is fixed at a level (leveling reset plane) where the wafer holder is adjusted so that it is parallel to the travel plane of the wafer stage. Leveling is not performed.

===== [p185-186] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Intrashot Multipoint Measurement EGA (for Computing the Shot Factors) (2块) =====
--[p185]-- When each individual shot shows its own characteristics in its expansion, contraction or rotation that differ from the changes in the shot array, it is possible to estimate the change of the shot’s characteristics by measuring three or more pairs of EGA marks (X and Y marks) in a shot. This process, where multiple pairs (one pair: one X mark and one Y mark) of EGA marks are measured in a shot, is referred to as intrashot multipoint measurement.
--[p186]-- When three or more pairs of EGA marks are measured in a shot, the following shot factors are measured in addition to the wafer factors (NOTES 1 and 2) .

===== [p306-306] 6.1.4 Determining the Possibility of Focusing (2块) =====
--[p306]-- Measured signals obtained from such points can hardly be expected to yield adequate focusing.
The area on the wafer in which focusing is impossible is normally within 3 mm (standard setting (NOTE) ) from the edge of the wafer. Depending on the state of the wafers for each process, focusing unfeasible range may be changed by Focusing Disable Range (p).
NOTE
The standard setting is determined for each machine.
--[p306]-- \~ Shot Criteria for Normal Focusing \~
In order to correctly perform focusing based on the measurement by the AF sensor, the shot must be located in the focusing feasible area where is inside the wafer edge. If the shot is on the edge of the wafer, the points measured by the AF sensor will lie on a non-uniform wafer surface or even off the wafer. Measured signals obtained from such points can hardly be expected to yield adequate focusing.

===== [p76-76] • For transfer alone (2块) =====
--[p76]-- Wafer transport for only transfer purposes (NOTE 2) can be classified into two different types:
Lot Pass The specified number of wafers are transferred. All wafers are considered to have been exposed normally.
Lot Reject The specified number of wafers are transferred. No wafers are considered to have been exposed normally.
--[p76]-- This kind of wafer transport is mainly used in inline specifications. The wafer, however, merely passes through the NSR, and no exposure is done on the wafer stage. Occasionally, this may also be used to collect the subsequent wafers in cases exposure processing has been interrupted.
For the local operation, wafer transport may be used for the case that various products are contained in the carrier and the middle part wafers are not exposed.

===== [p224-224] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (1) The Focusing Sensors Used in EGA Measurement and Execution Timing (3块) =====
--[p224]-- The focusing sensor to be used is only an AF sensor located at the shot center.
Taking into consideration the EGA measurement accuracy, it is the best way to perform focusing at each mark position. For this reason, when using the LSA sensor and the LIA sensor, which are the alignment sensors of on-axis lens, the measurement is performed after performing focusing at each mark position.
--[p224]-- In the case of the FIA sensor, however, since the AF sensor position (just under the projection lens center) is distant from the FIA sensor position (just under the wafer alignment microscope), moving the stage greatly at each mark measurement is needed in order to perform focusing at each mark position as well as the case of the LSA sensor and the LIA sensor. Therefore, focusing and timing when using the FIA sensor can be specified considering the measurement accuracy and throughput.
--[p224]-- (Focusing Mode of FIA-EGA (s))

===== [p36-36] 5.2 WAFER TRANSPORT UNIT (2块) =====
--[p36]-- In the wafer transport unit (NOTE) , the robot arm takes out a wafer from the wafer transfer site of the inline system or from the carrier set on the local carrier table. It then sets the wafer on the pre-alignment station and rotates it to adjust the orientation of the wafer. In that condition, the wafer is carried to the wafer stage by the load slider.
--[p36]-- In that condition, the wafer is carried to the wafer stage by the load slider.
After exposure is finished, the unload slider unloads the wafer from the wafer stage, and transfers it to the robot arm, which returns it to the transfer site of the inline system or to the local carrier (inside the NSR).
Wafers are placed in the wafer carrier and the carrier is set on the carrier table.

===== [p241-242] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (3) EGA Around Search (4块) =====
--[p242]-- If the number of times skipping has been performed in a row exceeds the number specified by Fine Alignment Error Skip Limit (s), an alignment error occurs.
An EGA shot count error occurs if the number of shots on which measurement has succeeded decreases because of alignment error skip and falls short of the value specified in EGA Requisite Shots (p).
--[p241]-- When measurement of an alignment mark in an EGA shot ends in failure (due to a mark detection error or some other alignment errors such as the exceeding of the tolerance), the around search function sets shots (around shots) located adjacent to the shot as substitute EGA shots and measures them instead.
--[p241]-- This function accomplishes two things: 1) it reduces the number of interruptions in processing caused by alignment errors, and 2) it prevents a decline in measurement accuracy due to a smaller number of successfully measured shots.
If the EGA around search function fails to measure the alignment marks in any of the substitute shots, the alignment error skip function starts.
--[p242]-- When EGA around search on a certain EGA shot fails to measure marks, the alignment error skip function automatically skips measurement of the shot and advances to the measurement of the next EGA shot without generating an error.
Specify by Fine Alignment Error Skip Limit (s) the upper limit of number of times alignment error skip is continuously performed.

===== [p245-245] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Automatic OF Retry (1块) =====
--[p245]-- When an error occurs during EGA execution and all of the possible error recovery functions are tried but fail to avoid an error (NOTE 1) , the automatic OF retry function automatically returns the wafer to the pre-alignment unit of the wafer loader, where it repeats alignment processing from pre-alignment\_1. This function is executed only when Alignment Error Recovery Mode (s) is set to “Assist” (NOTE 2) .

===== [p194-194] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > If g-EGA and t-EGA are executed for the purpose of correcting mark position for the final sensor (1块) =====
--[p194]-- The wafer factors and shot factors derived in t-EGA are used as the final correction factors. For convenience, the correction function comprising the final correction factors is expressed as Fdesign→EGA (this is the same as Fdesign→t-EGA).
For additional information on exposure in which these correction functions and correction factors are used, see Chapter 7, “Wafer Alignment and Exposure.”

===== [p130-130] (3) Function for Preventing Excessive Reticle Rotation (3块) =====
--[p130]-- The rotation check in reticle alignment check is performed to prevent heavy loads on reticle stage control in exposure, stemming from excessive reticle rotation. A rotation error occurs when the reticle rotation calculated in reticle alignment check exceeds Reticle Rotation Check Tolerance (s).
A rotation error occurs if the following formula is not satisfied: | Reticle rotation | ≤ | Reticle Rotation Check Tolerance (s) |
--[p130]-- Specifies the maximum number of times to repeat the rotation removal by rotating the reticle stage and reticle alignment check when a rotation error occurs in reticle alignment check.
--[p130]-- When the aforementioned rotation error occurs in reticle alignment check, the reticle stage is automatically rotated the number of times specified by Reticle Rotation Check Try Count (s) to remove the rotation. An error is reported to the operator after all the removal attempts repeated the specified number of times have failed to clear the excessive rotation.

===== [p258-258] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ◆ Shot Rot. Control by EGA (Shot) (1块) =====
--[p258]-- Shot rotation is corrected using the shot rotation factor computed in wafer alignment.
The control amount is determined by the following formula:
Control amount
= (Shot rotation factor derived in wafer alignment)
\+ (Wafer rotation factor derived in wafer alignment)
\+ (Rotation correction value specified by the user) (NOTE 2)

===== [p153-153] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > • Automatic assist mode (1块) =====

===== [p181-181] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (4) Automatic OF Retry (4块) =====
--[p181]-- Occasionally failure to orient the wafer properly in pre-alignment (due to erroneous detection of OF or notch) can result in a mark’s failure to enter the measurement range of an alignment sensor in search alignment.
--[p181]-- When an error occurs during the execution of wafer search alignment, and the error cannot be avoided even after all the possible recovery processing has been tried (NOTE) , the automatic OF retry function automatically returns the wafer to the wafer pre-alignment unit and repeats the alignment processing from pre-alignment.
NOTE
See “(5) Automatic Reject” in this section for details on situations in which an error cannot be avoided during execution of wafer search alignment.
--[p181]-- The automatic OF retry function is executed only when Alignment Error Recovery Mode (s) is set to “Assist”. The number of iterations for this function is specified by Automatic OF Retry Loop Limit (s). When Automatic OF Retry Loop Limit (s) is set to “0”, the automatic OF retry function is not executed.
If an error cannot be avoided even after automatic OF retry has been repeated the specified number of times, an error dialog to notify the user of it appears.
Recommended setting: “1”
--[p181]-- This parameter specifies how many times at the maximum automatic OF retry may be repeated.
◆ 0 No automatic OF retry is performed.
◆ 1 to 99 Automatic OF retry is performed the specified number of times at the maximum.
For additional information for Alignment Error Recovery Mode (s), see “(5) Automatic Reject” in this section.

===== [p31-31] 4.2 WAFER ALIGNMENT SYSTEM (2块) =====
--[p31]-- The wafer alignment system has the following three types of sensors to measure the marks on the wafer.
• LSA
• FIA (optional)
• LIA (optional)
The TTL optical system is used for LSA and LIA. On the other hand, the optical system of the off-axis microscope is used for FIA.
In the exposure of the second and subsequent layers, these sensors are used to measure the alignment marks laid down in the first layer. The results are used to precisely overlay a new exposure upon an existing field.
--[p31]-- The results are used to precisely overlay a new exposure upon an existing field.
To overlay shots precisely, the relative distance between a sensor and the center of the projection lens (baseline) must be determined.
Therefore, the baseline of each sensor is measured by using the fiducial marks provided on the wafer stage.

Now reticle unloading is completed.

===== [p240-240] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Automatic Remeasurement (1块) =====

===== [p191-191] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Combination of correction factors by two types of alignment sensors (2块) =====
--[p191]-- Erroneous result may be calculated to the specific correction factor by some sensors because of mark exposure condition or resist-coating condition.
For example, it is known that when marks, which have strong asymmetrical tendency on the edge of the wafer, are measured, the FIA is good at deriving the scaling factor while the LSA sensor is good at deriving the offset factor. This performance difference stems from differences in the measurement principles of each alignment sensor.
--[p191]-- In such a case, execute two stages of EGA using different types of sensors, and then select which result to use for each correction factor, g-EGA or t-EGA. (For details, see Chapter 7, “Wafer Alignment and Exposure.”)

===== [p75-75] 2. The robot arm receives the wafer. (1块) =====
--[p75]-- The robot arm receives the wafer from the unload slider.

===== [p46-46] • Resetting a reticle that has already been loaded (no loading or unloading) (2块) =====
--[p46]-- If it is NG (not good), it returns to the reticle case.
• Collecting a reticle that has finished being used (unloading)
The reticle that has finished being used is unloaded to its reticle case.
When an exposure command is launched, the parameter, Reticle Operation (c), is specified according to the purpose of the reticle transport operation.
--[p46]-- This operation is performed when the reticle already on the reticle stage is used in exposure. It may also be used to resume exposure processing when it has been interrupted.
There are two types of reticle resettings:
Alignment Only Reticle alignment is performed for the reticle.
From Particle Check The reticle on the stage undergoes particle check, and if it passes, reticle alignment is performed. If it is NG (not good), it returns to the reticle case.

===== [p230-230] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 6.8 ERROR HANDLING IN EGA EXECUTION (1块) =====

===== [p217-217] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (3) Mark Detection Algorithm Employed by the FIA Sensor (2块) =====
--[p217]-- Correct detection of a mark via signal processing requires selection of the appropriate signal processing conditions depending on the state of the exposed layer. The sequence of signal processing for mark detection using the FIA sensor is as follows:
1. The signal is subjected to smoothing over the range that will undergo signal processing.
This range is set in FIA Processing Gate Width B (p).
2. The signal peak is detected over the entire range.
--[p217]-- The signal peak is detected over the entire range.
It is checked whether the value of each peak satisfies the minimum contrast value required to recognize signals as marks. Peaks that do not meet this requirement are excluded as noise from signal. The contrast value (contrast limit) used in noise exclusion is specified using Contrast Limit (p).

===== [p316-316] [3] Leveling Offset (On) (c)/(p) (1块) =====
--[p316]-- Performs a test exposure to derive the amount of displacement in the inclination of the image plane when the leveling adjustment plane is the base. This displacement is unique to the process.

===== [p250-250] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Wafer Coordinates Adjustment (m) (2块) =====
--[p250]-- The function composed of this parameter and the wafer offset factor calculated from Wafer Pre-alignment Adjustment (m) is the first functions for correcting the coordinates (orthogonal coordinates having the wafer center as their origin) on the wafer.
In other words, the functions is used as the correction function for adjusting the shot layout in the first exposure and as the correction function for adjusting the measurement position in search alignment in the second exposure.
--[p250]-- In the second exposure, the correction function is updated successively based on the result of search alignment and EGA. Each updating improves the correction accuracy.

===== [p185-185] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Wafer rotation factor (2块) =====
--[p185]-- Wafer Scaling Factor
Wafer Orthogonality Factor
The shot positions finally estimated by EGA are those where the estimated shot positions for each of the four wafer factors are incorporated.
--[p185]-- The factor for the rotation, which is used to correctly overlay the current shot array onto the shot array of the previous layer.
• Wafer scaling factor
The factor for expansion or contraction, which is used to correctly overlay the current shot array onto the shot array of the previous layer.
• Wafer orthogonality factor
The factor for the XY orthogonality, which is used to correctly overlay the current shot array onto the shot array of the previous layer.
Wafer Rotation Factor

===== [p276-276] • Reticle rotation (1块) =====
--[p276]-- The reticle is rotated by moving the reticle stage by an amount equal to sum of the following values: the residual reticle rotation derived in reticle alignment check and the rotation factors computed in wafer alignment. This corrects the rotation of the shot shape.

===== [p230-230] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (1) Tolerance Check for EGA Measurement Results (1块) =====
--[p230]-- This function is performed to prevent misrecognition of an EGA mark. In this function, an alignment error is generated if the absolute value of the measurement results of an EGA mark in an EGA shot exceeds the absolute value of the tolerance. The tolerance is specified using EGA Result Allowance (p).

===== [p319-319] Shot Conditions When Normal Leveling Is Possible (1块) =====
--[p319]-- The position to be measured by the leveling sensor (NOTE) is within leveling feasible area.
• Focusing or shift focusing is possible.

===== [p174-174] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > 5.5.4 Techniques for Improving Mark Detection Accuracy (2块) =====
--[p174]-- When multiple signals having characteristics similar to those of the true mark signal appear in the search signal data, this parameter specifies which signal to select as the alignment mark signal.
--[p174]-- In search alignment, to improve mark detection rates, measurements are taken over a wide range. For this reason, the sensor system occasionally detects a similar signal to a mark signal erroneously in the area around the target mark.
When multiple signals likely to be a mark signal appear within the signal processing range, select one according to the specification by Search Mark Choice (p).
Standard setting: “Middle”

===== [p15-15] Step 3 Reticle Alignment Is Executed (1块) =====
--[p15]-- 1. The SRA and VRA sensors measure the alignment marks on the reticle to verify the reticle position with the center of the reduction projection lens as the origin. In an exposure of a second or subsequent layer (2nd exposure), the baselines (relative distances from the center of the reduction projection lens) of the wafer alignment sensors are verified in preparation for accurate wafer alignment.

--[p182]-- The automatic reject function automatically rejects (unloads) a wafer that causes a measurement error, and proceeds to the next wafer processing. This function starts only after every executable error recovery procedure has been tried but failed to avoid the error in search alignment.

(xi’,yi’) = Gpre (xi,yi) + (OWX,OWY) + (ORX,ORY) i= 1,2, ... , n (n: Number of shots),

===== [p170-170] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > Relevant Info 2: Difference in Weight Center Among Algorithms 6, 7, and 8 (2块) =====
--[p170]-- The weight center range of the signal intensity differs among algorithms 6,7, and 8 in that order. Algorithm 8 is the most effective for the low range of the signal intensity. In the example below, Group 1 is selected by algorithm 6, and Group 2 is selected by algorithm 7 or 8.
Algorithm 6 regards Group 1 as the mark signal. Algorithms 7 and 8 regard Group 2 as the mark signal.
–Algorithm 6
Weighted center at 40% level (of signal intensity)
–Algorithm 7
--[p170]-- Weighted center at 40% level (of signal intensity)
Weighted center at 30% level (of signal intensity)
–Algorithm 8
Weighted center at 20% level (of signal intensity)

===== [p187-187] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • 10-parameter model (2块) =====

--[p187]-- This model calculates both the wafer and shot factors.
In addition to the six types of wafer factors, this model calculates the following four types of shot factors:
\- Shot scaling factor X, Y (NOTE 1)
\- Shot rotation factor
\- Shot orthogonality factor (NOTE 2)
If Multipoint EGA (p) is set to “Head”, the 6-parameter model is used to compute only the wafer factors for the second and subsequent wafers (NOTE 3) .

===== [p235-235] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (4) EGA Limit Check (1块) =====
--[p235]-- This function is designed to prevent inappropriate correction factors from being calculated in EGA in such a case that an EGA mark is misrecognized. When the absolute value of one of the following correction factors exceeds the absolute value of the specified tolerance (EGA Correction Factor Tolerance (s)), an EGA limit error is generated (NOTE) .
• Wafer scaling factor
• Wafer orthogonality factor
• Shot rotation factor
• Shot scaling factor

===== [p52-52] 3.1.1 NG Reticle Report (2块) =====
--[p52]-- The user is notified of the check result if the outcome of reticle check is NG (failed).
If Reticle Identification Check Judgement Mode (s) and Reticle Particle Check Judgement Mode (s) are set to “Manual”, the outcome of the check is reported regardless of whether it is “passed” or “failed”. The user makes a final determination on what to do on the basis of the check result.
--[p52]-- The user makes a final determination on what to do on the basis of the check result.
If Reticle Identification Check Judgement Mode (s) and Reticle Particle Check Judgement (s) are set to “Full Auto.”, no information is given to the users even in NG.

===== [p267-268] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Correcting the Shot Magnification in the Second Layer (5块) =====
--[p267]-- This method ensures that the shots are completely overlaid the shots in the previous layer.
Since the expansion of a particular shot array usually matches that of each shot, it is sufficient to correct the lens magnification using the wafer scaling factor only.
If, however, the expansion of the shots somehow differs from that of the array, the magnification of the lens should be corrected using the shot scaling factors as well.
The correction amount of the shot magnification is determined according to the following formula:
Correction amount of the shot magnification = ∆Mw + ∆Ms,
--[p268 [表]]-- Factors that are not calculated are treated as zero.
For additional information on a factor calculated in each alignment, see Chapter 5, “Wafer Search Alignment," and Chapter 6, “EGA."For additional information on the method of adjusting the correction factors, see Section 7.2, “Adjusting the Correction Factors."
Table: Shot Magnification Correction in the Second Layer
Shot MagnificationControl Switch (s) | Shot MagnificationControl (p) | Factors Calculated in Alignment | User-Specified CorrectionValues
--[p267]-- In the exposure for the second and subsequent layers, the NSR determines the scaling factor in search alignment or EGA, and recognizes the scaling factor as the shot size of the previous layer. The magnification of the projection lens is adjusted so that the size of the shots on the current layer will be precisely matched to that of the previous layer, then exposure is performed. This method ensures that the shots are completely overlaid the shots in the previous layer.

===== [p98-98] • Wafer factors (2块) =====
--[p98]-- \- Wafer offset factor (shift in the shot array in the X and Y directions)
\- Wafer scaling factor (magnification or reduction in the shot array)
Wafer orthogonality factor (distortion in the rotation of the Y axis of the shot array)
Wafer Offset Factor
Wafer Rotation Factor
Wafer Scaling Factor
Wafer Orthogonality Factor
--[p98]-- Wafer factors are changes in the shot array caused by the expansion and deformation of a wafer that occur in subsequent processes as well as differences in loading between individual machines. Errors in the actual shot array with respect to the designed shot array are thought to have the following four components:
\- Wafer offset factor (shift in the shot array in the X and Y directions)
\- Wafer rotation factor (rotation of the shot array)

===== [p73-73] 2. The wafer is oriented at the pre-alignment unit. (1块) =====
--[p73]-- After receiving the wafer, the robot arm vacuum-fixes the wafer and transports it to the pre-alignment unit. There, the pre-alignment turntable vacuum-fixes the wafer and rotates. While it rotates, the circumference sensors and the OF notch sensors monitor the wafer and then stop it when the OF or notch of the wafer reaches the orientation specified in OF Direction (p). This operation is referred to as pre-alignment\_1.

===== [p194-194] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • If only g-EGA is performed (1块) =====
--[p194]-- The wafer factors and shot factors derived in g-EGA are used as the final correction factors. For convenience, the correction function comprising the final correction factors is expressed as Fdesign→EGA (this is the same as Fdesign→g-EGA).

===== [p96-96] 1.1.1 Exposure According to the Design Specification (1块) =====
--[p96]-- The first layer (the first exposure) is exposed according to the shot layout, corresponding the product design, specified in the process program.
To realize this objective, the reticle, which contains the circuit pattern for the IC layer, must be placed in exactly the correct attitude with respect to the stepping direction of the wafer stage.
This requires a method of measuring the attitude of the reticle and correcting any deviations in it. This process is called reticle alignment.

• Correction factor based on a design value (NOTE)
--[p227]-- The correction factors derived by EGA (g-EGA and t-EGA) are expressed as either of these two values:
A value obtained by adding an adjustment value specified with a machine adjustment parameter to a correction factor that is calculated based on a design value (NOTE)

===== [p324-325] 6.4.1 Principle of Focus Calibration (2块) =====
--[p325]-- In focus calibration, using the illumination light with the same wavelength as with exposure light, under surface of the reticle is illuminated through the FC pattern plate (NOTE 1) on the wafer stage. Then the reflected light is detected again through the FC pattern plate. The point where signal strength (light quantity) is maximum is regarded as the best focus (NOTE 2) .
--[p324]-- In focus calibration, by measuring signals from the AF sensor and TTL-FC2 together while scanning the Z stage up and down, the focal position difference (∆Z) between the positions determined from each measured signal is calculated.
In the following focusing operation, by adjusting the calculated focal position difference (∆Z) in terms of halving drive amount, the focus can be correctly adjusted to the best focus position.

--[p81]-- Wafers are supplied until the number reaches the setting of Number of Wafers (c)/(s).

--[p311]-- This is the shift focusing method applied when Shift Focusing Position (s) is set to “Intra-shot”.

===== [p326-326] 6.4.2 Focus Calibration Function and Execution Timing (2块) =====
--[p326]-- To ensure accurate focusing in exposure, the AF sensor must be adjusted so that it can detect the true best focus.
For this purpose, the following two focus calibration functions have been provided (NOTE 2) :
• Periodic management function for adjusting the focus of the machine
• Function for correcting short-term fluctuations in focus
NOTE
2. Focus calibration is executed when performing reticle multipoint measurement.
--[p326]-- Focus calibration is executed when performing reticle multipoint measurement.
The focus calibration for reticle multipoint measurement is executed only when Focus Calibration for RMPM (s) is set to “On”. Reticle multipoint measurement is available only for the NSR equipped with the Reticle Multipoint Measurement option.

===== [p16-16] Step 4 Wafer Is Exposed (4块) =====
--[p16]-- The wafer is exposed according to the settings of the shot map and the exposure condition data. When performing the exposure on the second or subsequent layers (2nd exposure), sample shots (s-shot or g-shot) are measured prior to exposure.
1. When performing the 2nd exposure, search marks in the s-shots are detected by the specified wafer alignment sensor.
--[p16]-- Exposure processing ends when the last wafer is exposed and is unloaded to its carrier.
--[p16]-- After aligning the wafer surface to the image plane derived by the AF sensor (focusing), wafer inclination is corrected according to the detection result of the leveling sensor (leveling).
4. The shot is exposed for the specified amount of time.
5. 2. through 4. are repeated until exposure finishes.
Step 4 (1. through 5.) is repeated, while exchanging wafers one after another, for precisely the specified number of wafers.
--[p16]-- In this exposure, EGA marks in g-shots are measured by the specified wafer alignment sensors with the aid of the results of search mark detection.
2. The stage is stepped to where the exposure position of the shot on the wafer is directly underneath the projection lens. In the 2nd exposure, the alignment results are reflected in the exposure position, orientation, and size of the shot.
3.

===== [p74-74] 1. The wafer is taken up the stage. (1块) =====

===== [p76-76] 2.1 PURPOSE OF WAFER LOADING AND UNLOADING (2块) =====
--[p76]-- There are two purposes in wafer transport:
• For exposure
In order for a wafer to undergo exposure on the wafer stage, the wafer is transported from its wafer carrier to the stage. After exposure completes, the wafer is returned to the wafer carrier. Transportation for exposure can be classified into three different types:
Normal Exposure The specified number of wafers are exposed. After each wafer finishes exposure, it is replaced with the next one.
--[p76]-- After each wafer finishes exposure, it is replaced with the next one.
This operation continues until all the wafers have been processed.
Load Only (NOTE 1) A single wafer is loaded onto the wafer stage.
Exposure Only Only the wafer on the stage undergo wafer alignment and exposure. Wafer transport is not performed.

===== [p103-103] 1.3.1 Configuration of the Alignment Optical System (2块) =====
--[p103]-- The alignment process uses both the sensors of the reticle alignment system and the sensors of the wafer alignment system.
• Sensors of the reticle alignment system
\- Scanning reticle alignment (SRA) sensor
\- Video reticle alignment (VRA) sensor
\- Image slit sensor (ISS) (NOTE)
• Sensors of the wafer alignment system
\- Laser step alignment (LSA) sensor
\- Field image alignment (FIA) sensor (NOTE)
\- Laser interferometric alignment (LIA) sensor (NOTE)
NOTE
--[p103]-- \- Laser interferometric alignment (LIA) sensor (NOTE)
In the NSR of i11 and i12 series, the FIA and LIA sensors are installed as options. In NSR-4425i, the FIA sensor is installed as an option, but the LIA sensor and ISS cannot be installed as options.

===== [p128-128] 3.2.2 Adjusting the Results of Reticle Alignment Check (3块) =====
--[p128]-- The VRA measurement results in reticle alignment check contain a displacement caused by fabrication error in the reticle alignment mark itself. Therefore, the reticle posture (reticle offset factor and reticle rotation factor) is computed from the measurement results with that displacement value portion removed.
The displacement of the reticle posture caused by fabrication error in the reticle alignment mark is corrected using Reticle Alignment Adjustment (p).
--[p128]-- In this case, the shift can be corrected with Reticle Rotation Adjustment (m).
In reticle alignment check this adjustment value is subtracted from the derived reticle rotation factor.
Specify a constantly existing reticle rotation factor with the sign reversed.
--[p128]-- Specify the fabrication error with the sign reversed for each mark of X (average of X1 and X2), Y (Y1), and θ (Y2). The values are specified after converted them into the values for the image projected on the wafer.
If a certain deviation exists in the reticle rotation factor calculated in reticle alignment check, and the exposure shift caused by this is clearly shown in the prior evaluation exposure, that deviation is considered to be an error in VRA measurement using the fiducial mark.

===== [p298-299] 6.1.1 Principle of Focusing (3块) =====
--[p299]-- For details, see Part IV, “Alignment” of this manual.
2. In NSR-4425i, only an AF sensor is installed at the center of a shot.
At each measurement point of the AF sensor, as illustrated in the following figure, a slit image is projected and formed onto the wafer surface with light from the halogen lamp and the AF sensor detector catches the reflected light. The displacement of the wafer surface from the focal plane is detected as a positional shift in the catoptric light at the detector.
--[p298]-- The following figure shows the AF sensor (NOTE 1) that is used in focusing at exposure on NSR of i11 series and NSR-4425i. The AF sensor is the lined multipoint AF sensor which has five diagonal measurement points in the exposure field (NOTE 2) . Normally, focusing is performed using an AF sensor located at the center of a shot.
NOTE
1. In case of performing focusing at alignment, only an AF sensor located at the center of the AF sensors is used.
--[p299]-- Each sensor of the AF sensor is adjusted so that the height of the wafer surface when the reflected light is detected at the center of the detector will be the best focus.

===== [p170-170] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > Relevant Info 1: Slice Interval for Each Algorithm (1块) =====
--[p170]-- Algorithms 6, 7, and 8 slice a signal at comparatively wide intervals between the peak and the bottom of the signal. Algorithm 10 slices the low intensity range of the signal at narrow intervals. Algorithm 11 slices the low and high intensity range of the signal at narrow intervals, and slices the middle range at wide intervals.

===== [p276-276] • Stepping (1块) =====
--[p276]-- Calculate the correct exposure position of a shot using the correction functions obtained in wafer alignment for each design exposure position (design value) determined by Wafer Basic Data (p) and Wafer Shot Map Data (p) of process program. The wafer stage then steps to the calculated exposure position of a shot, where it is exposed. The stage moves thereafter according to the shot exposure plan in due order (stepping).

===== [p58-58] (3) Method of Judging the Results of the Reticle Particle Check (2块) =====
--[p58]-- By specifying Number of Reticle Particle Check (s), the check can be performed twice or more.
In this case, the reticle is judged passed only when it has passed all the checks.
--[p58]-- Use Reticle Particle Check Judgment Mode (s) to specify whether the judgment on whether the reticle that has been inspected can be used is made by the system automatically or by the user.
In order to perform judgment automatically, the criterion, which decides whether to pass or fail, must be set with Judgment Level of Particle Check (Reticle) (s) and Judgment Level of Particle Check (Pellicle) (s).
By specifying Number of Reticle Particle Check (s), the check can be performed twice or more.

===== [p152-152] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > (3) Correction Factors Derived From Pre-alignment Assist (2块) =====

$$ \mathsf { F _ { d e s i g n p r e \_ a s s i s t } = F _ { p r e \_ a s s i s t } \circ F _ { d e s i g n a d j } } $$

===== [p78-78] [1] Carrier Selection (c)/(s) (2块) =====
--[p78]-- ◆ \* Displayed when Wafer Stream (c)/(s) is “\*”, and can not be selected.
◆ Extra Loads wafers from the local port “Extra” of the NSR (NOTE) .
NOTE
Only the type 2 wafer loader can use “Extra”, and this port is the extended carrier port besides the local ports, “Right” and “Left”.
Note that a parameter, Wafer Stream (c)/(s) does exist for specifying wafer load and unload route (which port a wafer exits from and returns to) of the NSR. When Wafer Stream (c)/(s) is specified, the setting of Carrier Selection (c)/(s) is automatically determined.
Therefore, in normal operation, you need not to specify the carrier port that supplies wafers by using Carrier Selection (c)/(s) (NOTE 1) .
--[p78]-- Specifies the carrier port that supplies wafers.
◆ Right Loads wafers from the local port “Right” of the NSR. Usually this refers to the carrier port to the right of the pre-alignment unit.
◆ Left Loads wafers from the local port “Left” of the NSR. Usually this refers to the carrier port to the left of the pre-alignment unit.
◆ Line Loads wafers from the carrier port of the inline equipment.
◆ \* Displayed when Wafer Stream (c)/(s) is “\*”, and can not be selected.

===== [p179-179] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > Automatic Extension of the Search Range (2块) =====
--[p179]-- When an error occurs in wafer search alignment, automatic remeasurement is performed as an automatic recovery process. If it fails to detect the mark, the search range (NOTE 1) is automatically extended, and measurement is retried.
In automatic extension of the search range, measurement is retried as the range over which a search is performed is extended according to the setting of Search Length Extension Try Count (s).
--[p179]-- Every time the NSR fails to detect the search mark, it multiplies the search range by the value specified with Extension Rate of Search Length (s) to set the new search range, and then retries mark measurement in the newly set range (NOTE 2) . If measurement cannot be conducted successfully after the specified number of iterations (the number of times specified by Search Length Extension Try Count (s)), around search starts.

===== [p215-215] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > LSA Processing Gate Width A, B (p) (2块) =====
--[p215]-- Normally 2 to 4 mis specified. | Specifies signal processing rangecentered on expected mark position.18.75% between peak and bottommust be included in this range.Normally 8 to 10 ∞m is specified.

===== [p261-261] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Correcting the Shot Rotation in the Second and Subsequent Layers (1块) =====
--[p261]-- In the second exposure, the NSR determines the shot orientation in search alignment or EGA, and recognizes that orientation as the shot orientation of the previous layer. The reticle stage is rotated so that the orientation of the shots on the current layer will be precisely matched to that of the previous layer, and then exposure is performed.
This method ensures that the shots are completely overlaid on the shots in the previous layer (NOTE) .

===== [p114-114] 2.1.1 Wafer Stage Coordinate System (2块) =====
--[p114]-- The wafer stage coordinate system is an orthogonal coordinate system that indicates the position of the wafer stage. Its origin is immediately below the center of the reduction projection store lens.
The position of the wafer stage is measured by the wafer stage interferometers (laser interferometers, two on the X axis and one on the Y axis). The results are converted to the wafer stage coordinate by using the function stored in NSR.
--[p114]-- The results are converted to the wafer stage coordinate by using the function stored in NSR.
The stage position is represented by where the center of the wafer holder is located in the wafer stage coordinate system. For example, when the center of the wafer holder is immediately below the center of the reduction projection lens, the stage is at the origin of the coordinate system (0, 0).

===== [p224-224] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 6.6.1 Focusing in EGA Measurement (1块) =====
--[p224]-- The surface of the EGA mark has to be in focus to achieve good measurement accuracy. For this reason, Focusing is performed immediately prior to EGA mark measurement. This section describes the following topics related to focusing:
• The focusing sensors used in EGA measurement and execution timing
• Focus settings in EGA measurement
• Leveling in EGA measurement

===== [p83-83] • Old mode (2块) =====
--[p83]-- Use this mode to resume the supply when the processing has been interrupted before all of the specified number of wafers are supplied.
And, also, this mode is used in cases that the wafers in the carrier are processed separately in several times.
From the information stored in memory about wafers in the carrier, the NSR can determine which wafer is to be next processed.
--[p83]-- If local port is set for supplying wafers, the carrier table drives down until the next unprocessed wafer in the carrier can be taken out, and wafer supply resumes. If the inline port is set for supplying wafers, NSR requires the inline equipment to resume the supply of wafers.
The operation mode of the carrier table is specified by using Carrier Mode (c)/(s).

===== [p222-222] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > [17] Both Ends of FIA Multiple Mark (p) (2块) =====
--[p222]-- Standard setting: “Included”
This parameter specifies whether to use the signals from the mark lines at both ends of a multimark to calculate mark position.
◆ Excluded The multimark-end signals are not used. After all of the peaks of an N-line multimark are detected, the peak waveforms of both ends are excluded, and the peak waveforms corresponding to the remainder of the lines (N-2) are used to calculate the mark position.
--[p222]-- ◆ Included All line signals of the multimark, including both ends, are used. All of the peak waveforms of an N-line multimark are used to calculate the mark position.

===== [p257-257] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 7.4 SHOT ROTATION CORRECTION (2块) =====
--[p257]-- Although the first and the second exposures use different methods of shot rotation correction, both of them make adjustment by rotating the reticle stage or wafer stage.
The method of controlling shot rotation correction is specified using Shot Rotation Control Switch (s).
--[p257]-- Basically, each shot on the wafer is exposed with its orientation being the same as of the shot arrays.
To do this, it is essential to correct the rotation of a shot.
In the first exposure, shots are oriented exactly according to the design specification, while in the second exposure, shots are oriented to accurately overlay the shots of the previous layer.

===== [p222-222] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > [16] Contrast Limit (p) (2块) =====
--[p222]-- This parameter specifies the minimum contrast value required to recognize signals as mark signals.
The maximum and minimum values of signal data obtained within the signal processing range of alignment mark signals (the setting of FIA Processing Gate Width B (p)) are 100% and 0% respectively. A signal whose peak value is lower than this parameter setting is regarded as noise and excluded from signals to be processed.
--[p222]-- Standard setting: “25” for XY separate meas. marks $\mathsf X = ^ { \mathrm { a } \mathrm { i } } 2 5 ^ { \mathrm { y } } , \mathsf Y = ^ { \mathrm { a } } 5 0 ^ { \mathrm { y } }$ for horizontal XY simul. meas. marks X=“50”, Y=“25” for vertical XY simul. meas. marks

===== [p24-24] 3.3 WAFER STAGE UNIT (1块) =====
--[p24]-- The wafer stage unit performs highly accurate positioning at a high speed.

===== [p210-210] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (1) Averaging of Measurements Taken by the LSA Sensor (2块) =====
--[p210]-- In EGA measurement using the LSA system, a measurement mark can be scanned several times (multiscan), which results in averaging the measurement. In this case, the final measurement result is the average of the measurement results taken in these scans. Specify how many times scanning is repeated for averaging using Scanning Times (p).
--[p210]-- Specify how many times scanning is repeated for averaging using Scanning Times (p).
When a mark is scanned multiple times (multiscan), scanning can be performed at narrow intervals before and after the mark center position over the specified width. This eliminates variation in the measurement results due to differences in the scan position, That variation is shown in such a case that the configuration of the mark is distorted. Specify the interval (step width) when multiscanning is performed, using Scanning Steps (p).

===== [p249-249] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (1) Correcting the Deviation in the Correction Factors for the First Layer (2块) =====
--[p249]-- Each NSR has its own idiosyncrasies in how its wafer stage steps. Thus, for exposure of the first layer, their shot arrays are slightly different among NSRs. In such cases, it is a good idea to measure beforehand the difference among the machines and set this value in Wafer Coordinates Adjustment (m). This will provide a uniform first exposure on any NSR. To validate Wafer Coordinates Adjustment (m), specify “On” for Parameter Validity (Wafer Coordinates) (m).

===== [p99-99] • Shot factors (1块) =====
--[p99]-- These consist of variations in the form of the shot itself that occur because of reticle rotation, reticle expansion and slight differences between individual machines in the projection magnification of the reduction projection lens. Errors in the actual shot with respect to the designed shot are thought to have the following two components:
\- Shot rotation factor (rotation of the shot)
\- Shot scaling factor (magnification or reduction of the shot)
Shot Rotation Factor
Shot Scaling Factor

===== [p266-266] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ◆ Mag. Control by P.P. Value (1块) =====
--[p266]-- The wafer scaling factor and shot scaling factor computed in wafer alignment are not used. Shot magnification control is implemented using only the scaling correction value (NOTE 2) specified by the user.

The conditions necessary for applying this model are the same as those for the 6-parameter model.

===== [p172-172] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > [3] Search Signal Processing Algorithm (FIA) (p) (2块) =====
--[p172]-- Recommended setting: “41”
Specifies the signal processing algorithm for detecting the mark position from the captured signal data. Select a setting that gives the best performance with respect to the signal intensity and noise status.
◆ 40 Standard algorithm; it is used when the mark is easily identified. (For a standard signal)
◆ 41 It is used when uneven waves appears frequently and thus it is difficult to identify the mark. (For an uneven signal)
--[p172]-- ◆ 41 It is used when uneven waves appears frequently and thus it is difficult to identify the mark.
◆ 42 It is used when mark signal intensify is extremely weak compared with false signals (10% or less) (For a weak signal)
Standard Signal
Uneven Signal
Weak Signal

===== [p231-231] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > EGA Result Allowance (p) (1块) =====
--[p231]-- Recommended setting : “0.3” [µm] for FIA and LSA, and “1.5” [µm] for LIA
If the measurement result does not satisfy the formula below, an alignment error will occur.
| Measurement result for each mark | ≤ | EGA Result Allowance (p) |
The allowance must be set for each sensor using the following parameters: LSA Result Allowance (p), FIA Result Allowance (p), or LIA Result Allowance (p) (NOTE) .

===== [p328-328] (2) Function for Correcting Short-term Fluctuations in Focus (2块) =====
--[p328]-- This focus calibration function corrects short-term fluctuations in focus using the exposure commands (EXECUTE, CONTINUE, or READY command).
Since focus calibration depends on the reticle, performing focus calibration using the reticles in each process by these commands is not generally recommended.
--[p328]-- When performing focus calibration with these commands, analyze the reticle-depending rate among the focus calibration result beforehand with regard to the reticle to be used, check that the rate is within the permissible range, and then perform focus calibration.
The EXECUTE, CONTINUE, or READY command executes focus calibration on the timing described below.
• On reticle alignment
• On wafer exposure

===== [p335-335] Execution Restriction of Focus Calibration Measurement (1块) =====
--[p335]-- To prevent deterioration of measurement accuracy caused by imaging system, execution sequence is automatically changed in the focus calibration on the condition of some specific imaging system.
Specific illumination system condition mentioned here is the case of using illumination ID of the small σ-illumination with the illumination system NA value set to less than 0.29 (NOTE) .

===== [p258-258] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ◆ Shot Rot. Control by EGA (Wafer) (1块) =====
--[p258]-- Shot rotation is corrected using the wafer rotation factor computed in wafer alignment.
The control amount is determined by the following formula:
Control amount
= (Wafer rotation factor derived in wafer alignment)
\+ (Rotation correction value specified by the user) (NOTE 2)
The shot rotation factor computed in wafer alignment is not used.

--[p137]-- When a preliminary evaluation exposure reveals a fixed measurement displacement for each measurement in wafer alignment, the displacement can be considered as a measurement error of baseline check. Such an error is compensated by using Baseline Offset Adjustment (m).

===== [p254-254] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (1) Shot Position Correction for the First Layer (2块) =====
--[p254]-- To avoid this, the following factors are used in calculating the wafer stage position at exposure of each shot: 1) the wafer offset factor calculated from Wafer Pre-alignment Adjustment (m) and 2) the reticle offset factor derived in the reticle alignment check.
Wafer coordinate system before correction and shot position Wafer coordinate system corrected by the adjustment function calculated from Wafer Pre-alignment Adjustment (m) and shot position Shot position when wafer stage position is corrected by using reticle offset factor derived in reticle alignment check
--[p254]-- In the first exposure, shots are exposed exactly according to the layout specified in the design.
The setting error for the reticle and wafer loaded on the reticle and wafer stages respectively, however, will adversely affect the relationship between the wafer and the shot arrays exposed on it.

===== [p124-124] 3.1.3 Reticle Alignment Execution Mode (1块) =====
--[p124]-- In the reticle search and reticle fine alignment, automatic measurement using SRA is normally conducted. This is referred to as “automatic mode.”
If the reticle alignment cannot be preformed automatically, the user can execute the reticle alignment by manually moving the reticle stage with the operation panel while viewing the ITV screen. This is called “ manual mode.”
The execution mode for reticle search and reticle fine alignment is specified by Reticle Alignment Mode (s).

===== [p123-123] 3.1.2 Marks to Be Used in Reticle Alignment (2块) =====
--[p123 [表]]-- Specifies the type of reticle to be used.

--[p123]-- Reticle search and reticle fine alignment use the position of the reticle alignment marks on the reticle.
The positions of the reticle alignment marks on the reticle are set in a reticle type file. Reticle type files are provided according to reticle size, reticle alignment mark position and its form, and reflectance.
These reticle type files can be referred to by specifying Reticle Type (p) in process program.

--[p144]-- In NSR-4425i, since the simultaneous baseline check is not supported for the fiducial mark of this machine type, the non-simultaneous baseline check is the standard sequence.

Control amount

===== [p182-182] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Situations in Which an Error Cannot Be Avoided in Search Alignment (1块) =====
--[p182]-- When an error occurs in wafer search alignment, there are three situations in which an error cannot be avoided despite having tried every executable error recovery procedure:

===== [p153-153] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > • All wafers assist mode (1块) =====
--[p153]-- Pre-alignment assist is performed in manual mode for all wafers. Each pre-alignment assist result is reflected in the measurement target position in search alignment for each wafer.

===== [p97-97] 1.1.2 Overlay Exposure on a Preceding Layer (1块) =====
--[p97]-- The second and subsequent layers (2nd exposure) must be correctly exposed to match the exposure conditions of the previous layers.
To realize this, stepping must be adjusted to match the shot position of the previous layer, and the attitude of the reticle (circuit pattern) must be adjusted to match the shot orientation of the previous layer.
This requires a method of measuring the conditions of the shot array on the wafer and exposing on the basis of the measurement result.
This process of wafer measurement and overlay is called wafer alignment.

===== [p276-276] • Focusing and leveling (1块) =====
--[p276]-- The height and inclination of the wafer stage are adjusted to where the height of the wafer surface matches the height of the focal plane of the reduction projection lens and the inclination of the wafer surface matches the inclination of the image plane of the reduction projection lens.

===== [p81-81] • Unloaded wafer count mode (1块) =====
--[p81]-- Wafers continue to be supplied until the number of collected wafers reaches the setting of Number of Wafers (c)/(s).
The count includes the wafers which have gone out to NSR, if any.

===== [p70-70] Chapter 1 OVERVIEW OF WAFER TRANSPORT (1块) =====
--[p70]-- This chapter gives an overview of the sequence of wafer transport.

===== [p168-168] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > 5.5 SEARCH ALIGNMENT SENSORS AND MEASUREMENT (1块) =====
--[p168]-- Two alignment sensors, LSA and FIA (NOTE) , can be used for measurement in search alignment.
The main wafer alignment sensor (the sensor normally used in measurement) is the LSA sensor. FIA is especially good for detecting marks on rough wafer surfaces.
The alignment sensor used in search alignment is specified by Search Alignment Sensor (p).
NOTE
The FIA sensor is optional.

===== [p208-208] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Lower height marks (1块) =====
--[p208]-- The LSA sensor works well with lower height mark; however, it cannot be applied for some layers. In such cases, try to use the LIA sensor. Do not use the LSA sensor on wafers with lower height marks and extremely rough surfaces.
These criteria for sensor selection are summarized in the following figure.
After deciding which sensor to use, based on the above selection criteria, specify that sensor in EGA Sensor (p) (g-EGA Sensor (p) and t-EGA Sensor (p)).

===== [p73-73] 3. The wafer is loaded onto the stage. (1块) =====
--[p73]-- The load slider receives the wafer finished pre-alignment\_1, vacuum-fixes and transports it to the position where the wafer stage can receive it. The stage drives to immediately under the load slider, and the center table moves up in the Z direction to receive the wafer. As soon as the wafer is placed onto the center table, the center table drives down in the Z direction, and the wafer holder vacuum-fixes the wafer.
Now wafer load is completed.

===== [p62-62] Reticle Name Delimiter (s) (1块) =====
--[p62]-- Sequence Mode
For the reticle name specified in the process program or in RLAT, the character string after the character specified by this parameter is regarded as a comment.
◆ One alphanumeric or special character (excluding wildcards) !

===== [p146-146] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > 5.1 PRE-ALIGNMENT ASSIST (2块) =====
--[p146]-- \~ Determining the Search Alignment Measurement Position \~
In a pre-alignment assist operation, the user indicates the measuring position of the search marks so that they can be measured in search alignment.
The position where a wafer is loaded onto the wafer stage and how to control the wafer stage varies slightly depending on the machine. This variation is eliminated, however, in later measurement processes, so that there is no effect on exposure.
--[p146]-- Nevertheless, on rare occasions, in exposure processes incorporating several NSRs, an error may occur during measurement in search alignment as a result of this variation in accuracy among NSRs. Pre-alignment assist is designed to cope with such situations.

===== [p322-322] [1] Alignment Error Recovery Mode (s) (1块) =====
--[p322]-- Specifies the mode used to recover from an unavoidable error situation that occurs during focusing or leveling.
◆ Assist Recovery is achieved by means of an assist operation by the user.
◆ Reject The wafer on the wafer stage is rejected (retrieved to the carrier exclusively for NG wafers to be retrieved to), and processing continues with the next wafer.

===== [p251-251] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Wafer Orthogonality Correction (p) (1块) =====
--[p251]-- Specifies a correction value for the wafer orthogonality factor. Set a positive value for the direction that the Y axis tilts counterclockwise.

===== [p78-78] (1) Selection for the Carrier Port That Supplies Wafers (1块) =====
--[p78]-- Select the carrier port that supplies wafers (from a local port or inline port) using Carrier Selection (c)/(s).

===== [p146-146] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > \~ Rough Wafer Alignment \~ (1块) =====
--[p146]-- Search alignment is conducted on a wafer on which the second or subsequent layer is exposed (a second exposure wafer). This chapter describes the function and purpose of search alignment.

===== [p129-129] (1) Function for Preventing Mark Misrecognition in Measurement (1块) =====
--[p129]-- The tolerance check for reticle alignment check is performed to prevent misrecognition of the mark. A measurement error occurs if the measurement result exceeds the value specified by Reticle Alignment Tolerance (s).
Standard setting: “5.0” [µm]
A measurement error occurs if the following formula is not satisfied. The values are specified after converted then into the value for the image projected on the wafer.
| Measurement result | ≤ | Reticle Alignment Tolerance (s) |

===== [p96-96] 1.1 PURPOSE OF ALIGNMENT (1块) =====
--[p96]-- The alignment in the NSR is the processing performed to ensure that shots are exposed on the wafer in the proper position and form.
Alignment is performed to achieve two types of exposure:
• Exposure according to the design specification
• Overlay exposure on a preceding layer

===== [p311-311] • Shot that is partially within focusing feasible area (1块) =====
--[p311]-- Shift focusing is performed. Focusing is performed at a point within focusing feasible area in a shot. After that, the wafer is moved back to its original position before shift focusing, and then exposure is performed.

===== [p213-213] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Relevant Info 2: Excluding Both Ends of a Multimark (1块) =====
--[p213]-- Occasionally the signal waveforms at the ends of a multimark become exceedingly distorted due to the pattern density and the coverage of the resist.
In such cases, the mark line signals at both ends of a multimark can be excluded from signal processing by specifying “Excluded” in Both Ends of LSA Multiple Mark (p).

===== [p45-45] 2. Reticle is transferred to the slide carrier. (1块) =====

===== [p44-44] 5. The reticle is loaded onto the stage. (1块) =====
