===== [p208-208] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Asymmetrical marks (1块) =====
--[p208]-- The FIA sensor works well with asymmetrical marks; however, it cannot be applied for some layers. In such cases, try to use the LIA sensor. In the case of using the LIA sensor, using the mark with thin-slots is recommended. Do not use the LSA sensor on wafers with extremely rough surfaces.

===== [p251-251] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Correcting the Deviation in the Correction Factors for the Second and Subsequent Layers (2块) =====
--[p251]-- This will compensate this deviation.
In addition, since the conditions of a resist will vary from layer to layer, overlay accuracy can vary slightly between layers. In this case, it is convenient to measure this difference beforehand and set the value Alignment Correction (p). This will ensure a uniform overlay accuracy between any two layers.
--[p251]-- In the second and subsequent layers, using the correction factors derived in alignment without any changes to expose a wafer can produce a regular error (deviation) in the shot overlay (position, orientation, and size). In such cases, it is a good idea to measure this deviation beforehand using an overlay measuring equipment or the MEA-SURE command in the NSR and set this value in Alignment Correction (p). This will compensate this deviation.

===== [p288-288] • Stepping route (1块) =====
--[p288]-- This refers to the basic stepping route that is followed in exposing shots. The wafer stage steps along rows or columns of shots on the wafer shot map.

===== [p217-217] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (2) Averaging of the Image Data Captured by the FIA Sensor (1块) =====
--[p217]-- In measurement using the FIA sensor, image data corresponding to the number of scanning lines are captured. Among the scanning lines used for image capturing, specify the number of scanning lines to be used in signal processing with the Number of Line Averaging (p).
Image data corresponding to the specified number of scanning lines will be averaged and then used in signal processing.
Recommended setting: “56”
This parameter specifies the number of scanning lines used for averaging measured data.

===== [p279-279] Blind ID (1块) =====
--[p279]-- Specifies the blind setting for each shot. Specifying blind data for each blind ID makes it possible to expose a wafer with changing the area of the pattern on the reticle to be copied and printed onto the wafer, or with arbitrarily arranging multiple patterns on the same reticle.
The following sections explain three types of shot IDs: exposure, offset, and blind.

===== [p269-269] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > \~ Correction for Canceling Reticle Expansion \~ (1块) =====
--[p269]-- Reticle elasticity control adjusts the magnification of the lens to compensate for the expansion and contraction of a reticle caused by exposure light irradiation. The amount of reticle elasticity is estimated from several conditions including the amount of exposure light energy, reticle transmittance, and reticle reflectance (NOTE) . The estimated value is then used to control elasticity.

===== [p198-198] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > (4) EGA Execution Mode (Automatic or Manual) (1块) =====
--[p198]-- Typically EGA measurement is executed automatically by the alignment sensors. This is called the automatic mode. Two other modes are also available in EGA: the EGA assist mode, in which the user verifies the mark which has been detected by the sensor, and the manual mode used when the mark cannot be detected by a sensor. Select the desired mode for g-EGA and t-EGA respectively using EGA Switch (s) (g-EGA Switch (s) and t-EGA Switch (s)).

===== [p324-324] 6.4 FOCUS CALIBRATION (1块) =====
--[p324]-- The AF sensor is adjusted so that the correct focal plane of the reduction projection lens can be determined based on its measured signals. However, the measured signals slightly changes with time. In order to correct the change, periodic calibration of sensors is carried out. The processing is called focus calibration.

===== [p142-142] 4.2 CONDITIONS FOR EXECUTING BASELINE CHECK > (1) Static Simultaneous Baseline Check (1块) =====
--[p142]-- The wafer stage is moved so that the fiducial mark can be positioned just under the static reticle alignment sensor, “VRA sensor”, and the static wafer alignment sensors, “FIA sensor” and “LIA sensor”. Then the baseline check is executed with the stage
stopped.
By means of this baseline check method, more accurate baselines of the FIA and LIA sensors are obtained.

===== [p129-129] 3.2.3 Error Handling in Reticle Alignment Check Execution (1块) =====

===== [p13-13] Step 1 Reticle Is Loaded onto the Reticle Stage (1块) =====
--[p13]-- 1. A reticle is taken out from its reticle case in the specified reticle slot and loaded onto the reticle stage. Depending on the settings of sequence switches, the reticle may undergo particle check and ID check on its way to the stage.
Reticle Case → Robot Arm Fork → Side Carrier → Load Slider →Stage

===== [p254-254] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 7.3 SHOT POSITION CORRECTION (1块) =====
--[p254]-- In the first exposure, shots are exposed exactly in the design position. In the second exposure, shots are exposed to precisely overlay the shots in the previous layer.
Although the method of deriving the shot exposure position differs between the first exposure and the second exposure, the position is adjusted in both cases by driving the wafer stage a certain distance.
The stage driving distance to adjust the shot position

===== [p43-43] 2. The reticle is vacuum-fixed in the slide carrier. (1块) =====
--[p43]-- The robot arm fork with a reticle ascends to the position for transferring the reticle to the slide carrier. The slide carrier receives the reticle, and then vacuum-fixes it. In the slide carrier, positioning of the reticle (pre-alignment) is performed.
When the execution of reticle particle check is instructed subsequently, the reticle is transported to the reticle particle check position after pre-alignment (NOTE) .

===== [p250-250] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > Wafer Scaling Adjustment (m) (1块) =====
--[p250]-- Specifies the adjustment value for wafer scaling factor.

===== [p244-244] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > [12] Alignment Error Recovery Mode (s) (1块) =====
--[p244]-- Specifies the recovery method to be used when an error cannot be avoided in alignment execution.
◆ Assist Implements an assist operation by the user (NOTE) .

===== [p248-248] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > \~ Application of Wafer Alignment Results \~ (1块) =====
--[p248]-- This chapter describes how each correction factor derived in wafer alignment is used in exposure.

===== [p322-322] 6.3.2 Automatic Reject (1块) =====
--[p322]-- \~ Function Preventing Interruptions in Processing Due to Errors \~
When either of the errors shown below occurs during focusing or leveling in exposure, the wafer on the wafer stage is automatically rejected, and processing of the next wafer is started uninterrupted.
Automatic reject function prevents interruptions in processing when the errors mentioned below occur.

===== [p197-197] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > ◆ Normal (1块) =====
--[p197]-- The X and Y marks in one EGA shot are measured successively. When intrashot multipoint measurement is conducted, an X mark and a Y mark in a shot are measured alternately.
◆ Y→X All of the Y marks in all of the EGA shots are measured before any of the X marks is measured.
◆ X→Y All of the X marks in all of the EGA shots are measured before any of the Y marks is measured.
◆ Optimize (Not used.)

===== [p212-212] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • Maximum gradient method (algorithm 2) (1块) =====
--[p212]-- This method finds a level where the gradient of the signal waveform is steepest below the specified slice level. This level is set as the final slice level, and the mark center is calculated by slicing the waveform with the final slice level.

===== [p209-209] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > [1] EGA Sensor (p) (1块) =====
--[p209]-- ◆ FIA
FIA sensor
◆ LSA LSA sensor
◆ LIA LIA sensor
◆ TTR-LIA (Not used.)

===== [p300-300] 6.1.2 Plane Set by Focusing (1块) =====
--[p300]-- \~ Best Focus \~
The AF sensor is adjusted so that the correct focal plane for the reduction projection lens can be derived from its measurement results. However, these results can change gradually over time. To compensate this change, it is necessary to periodically calibrate (NOTE) the AF sensor, through a process called focus calibration. For information about focus calibration, see Section 6.4, “Focus Calibration.”

===== [p239-239] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > 6.8.2 Functions for Preventing Alignment Errors from Interrupting Processing (1块) =====
--[p239]-- EGA maintains alignment accuracy by excluding anomalous data from calculations with various check functions. The drawback of this is that these check functions sometimes generate an error that interrupts processing. The following functions have been provided to prevent this from happening:
• Multimark detection error reject
• Automatic remeasurement
• EGA around search
• Alignment error skip
• Automatic reject
• Automatic OF retry

===== [p191-191] 5.7 ERROR PROCESSING IN WAFER SEARCH ALIGNMENT > • More accurate positioning of alignment mark for the use of the final sensor (1块) =====
--[p191]-- In the case of using the sensor which requires severe mark positioning accuracy, such as the LIA sensor, EGA using the FIA sensor or LSA sensor should be performed in advance.
Also in this case, performing two stages of EGA is better.
In any cases when a two-stage EGA is conducted, set EGA Path (p) to “Both”. For additional information, see Section 6.3, “EGA Execution Conditions.”
